# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Counts the total quantity of print-format book items in the order, only if the order is completed.
Algorithm:
  If this.status != COMPLETED: Return 0
  count = 0
  For each line in this.itemLines:
    If line.libraryItem is BookItem AND line.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
      count += line.quantity
  Return count
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: BookItem, BookItemType, ItemLine, OrderStatus
Input: None
Output: int: Total quantity of print-format books, or 0 if order not completed.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns the calculated count.

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Transitions an order to completed status and updates the status of associated items.
Algorithm:
  this.status = COMPLETED
  For each line in this.itemLines:
    If line.libraryItem.status == HOLD AND (line.libraryItem is BookItem(PRINT_FORMAT) OR DigitalItem(DISC)):
      line.libraryItem.status = LOAN
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input: None
Output: void
Pre-condition: Order should be in PENDING status.
Post-condition: Order status is set to COMPLETED and eligible items are set to LOAN.

Order::addItemLine
Summary: Adds a new item line to a pending order and updates the library item status if applicable.
Algorithm:
  If this.status != PENDING OR itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE: Return false
  If exists item in this.itemLines where item.libraryItem == itemLine.libraryItem: Return false
  If itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem(PRINT_FORMAT) OR DigitalItem(DISC):
    itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD
  this.itemLines.add(itemLine)
  Return true
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input: itemLine (ItemLine): The item line to be added to the order.
Output: boolean: True if addition is successful, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Order status must be PENDING; LibraryItem must be AVAILABLE.
Post-condition: If successful, item line is added to itemLines and item status updated to HOLD if print/disc.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary: Removes an item line from a pending order and updates the associated library item status.
Algorithm:
  If this.status != PENDING: Return -1
  If itemLine exists in this.itemLines:
    If itemLine.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
      itemLine.libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE
    this.itemLines.remove(itemLine)
  Return this.itemLines.size()
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input: itemLine (ItemLine): The item line to remove.
Output: int: Updated count of item lines, or -1 if order is completed.
Pre-condition: Order must be in PENDING status.
Post-condition: Item line removed, library item status reset if previously HOLD, returns new count.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Counts the total quantity of print-format book items in the order, only if the order is completed.
Algorithm:
  If this.status != COMPLETED: Return 0
  count = 0
  For each line in this.itemLines:
    If line.libraryItem is BookItem AND line.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
      count += line.quantity
  Return count
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: BookItem, BookItemType, ItemLine, OrderStatus
Input: None
Output: int: Total quantity of print-format books, or 0 if order not completed.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns the calculated count.

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Transitions an order to completed status and updates the status of associated items.
Algorithm:
  this.status = COMPLETED
  For each line in this.itemLines:
    If line.libraryItem.status == HOLD AND (line.libraryItem is BookItem(PRINT_FORMAT) OR DigitalItem(DISC)):
      line.libraryItem.status = LOAN
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input: None
Output: void
Pre-condition: Order should be in PENDING status.

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total number of items associated with a specific digital audio item across all completed orders, considering only downloadable options.
Algorithm: 
  total = 0
  For each order in orders:
    If order.status == COMPLETED:
      For each line in order.itemLines:
        If line.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND line.libraryItem.type == AUDIO AND line.libraryItem.option == DOWNLOADABLE:
          total += line.quantity
  Return total
Related Features: option, type
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, DigitalItem, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus
Input: orders (List<Order>): A list of all orders in the system.
Output: int: Total count of downloads, 0 if none found.
Pre-condition: Input orders list must not be null.
Post-condition: Returns the cumulative quantity of the specific audio item across valid completed orders.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operations
+ DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
+ Order::addItemLine
+ Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
+ Order::handleOrder
+ Order::removeItemLine

3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::addItemLine
Summary: Adds a new item line to an order if the order is pending and the item is available.
Algorithm:
1. Check if Order.status == PENDING. If not, return false.
2. Check if itemLine.libraryItem.status == AVAILABLE. If not, return false.
3. Check if any existing itemLine in Order.itemLines has the same libraryItem. If so, return false.
4. Add itemLine to Order.itemLines.
5. If (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND type == PRINT_FORMAT) OR (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND option == DISC):
   - Set itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD.
6. Return true.
Related Features: status, itemLines, quantity, libraryItem.
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption.
Input: itemLine (ItemLine): The item line to add to the order.
Output: boolean: True if added successfully, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Order must exist; itemLine must be non-null.
Post-condition: If successful, itemLine is added to itemLines; library item status may be updated to HOLD.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary: Removes an item line from a pending order and resets the library item status if applicable.
Algorithm:
1. If Order.status == COMPLETED, return -1.
2. If itemLine exists in Order.itemLines:
   a. Remove itemLine from Order.itemLines.
   b. If itemLine.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
      - Set itemLine.libraryItem.status = AVAILABLE.
   c. Return Order.itemLines.size().
3. Return -1 if itemLine not found.
Related Features: status, itemLines, libraryItem.
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus.
Input: itemLine (ItemLine): The item line to be removed.
Output: int: The new count of item lines after removal, or -1 if failed.
Pre-condition: Order must be in PENDING status.
Post-condition: itemLine is removed; library item status is reset if it was HOLD.

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Finalizes an order by updating its status and the status of contained library items.
Algorithm:
1. Set Order.status = COMPLETED.
2. For each itemLine in Order.itemLines:
   a. If itemLine.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
      - If itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem AND type == PRINT_FORMAT:
        - Set itemLine.libraryItem.status = LOAN.
      - Else if itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem AND option == DISC:
        - Set itemLine.libraryItem.status = LOAN.
Related Features: status, itemLines, libraryItem, type, option.
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption.
Input: None.
Output: void.
Pre-condition: Order should be in PENDING status.
Post-condition: Order status is COMPLETED; library items previously on HOLD (print/disc) are now on LOAN.

Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Counts the total number of print-format book items in an order if the order is completed.
Algorithm:
1. If Order.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
2. Initialize count = 0.
3. For each itemLine in Order.itemLines:
   a. If itemLine.libraryItem is an instance of BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
      - count += itemLine.quantity.
4. Return count.
Related Features: status, itemLines, libraryItem, type, quantity.
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus.
Input: None.
Output: int: Total quantity of print-format books (>= 0).
Pre-condition: Order status must be COMPLETED to return a non-zero value.
Post-condition: Returns the calculated sum of quantities.

You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

```plantuml

title olrs Model

    type : BookItemType
}

}

    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean

}

    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}

    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}

    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}

    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}

    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total download count for a specific Audio digital item across all completed orders.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
2. For each order in orders:
   a. If order.status == COMPLETED:
      i. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
         - If itemLine.libraryItem is an instance of DigitalItem:
           - If itemLine.libraryItem.type == AUDIO AND itemLine.libraryItem.option == DOWNLOADABLE:
             - totalDownloads += itemLine.quantity.
3. Return totalDownloads.
Related Features: itemLines, quantity, libraryItem, type, option.
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, DigitalItem, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus.
Input: orders (List<Order>): A list of all system orders.
Output: int: The total count of downloads (>= 0).
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: Returns the aggregate quantity of all applicable item lines.

Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. ## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total number of downloads for a specific audio digital item across all completed orders.
Algorithm: 
  total = 0
  if this.type == AUDIO and this.option == DOWNLOADABLE:
    for each order in orders:
      if order.status == COMPLETED:
        for each line in order.itemLines:
          if line.libraryItem == this:
            total += line.quantity
  return total
Related Features: type, option
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus
Input: orders (List<Order>): A list of all orders in the system.
Output: total (int): The sum of quantities of this item in qualifying orders; 0 if none.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: The system state remains unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Returns the count of print-format book items in the order if completed.
Algorithm: 
  if this.status != COMPLETED: return 0
  count = 0
  for each line in this.itemLines:
    if line.libraryItem is BookItem and line.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
      count += line.quantity
  return count
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, BookItem, BookItemType, OrderStatus
Input: None
Output: count (int): Total count of print-format book items, or 0 if not completed or none found.
Pre-condition: None.
Post-condition: System state remains unchanged.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary: Removes an item line from an existing order and resets item status if necessary.
Algorithm: 
  if this.status == COMPLETED: return -1
  item = itemLine.libraryItem
  if this.itemLines.contains(itemLine):
    this.itemLines.remove(itemLine)
    if item.status == HOLD:
      item.status = AVAILABLE
  return this.itemLines.size()
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input: itemLine (ItemLine): The item line to be removed.
Output: count (int): The updated count of item lines, or -1 if failed.
Pre-condition: Order status must be PENDING.
Post-condition: Item line removed; library item status reset to AVAILABLE if it was HOLD.

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Updates order status to completed and updates library item statuses.
Algorithm: 
  this.status = COMPLETED
  for each line in this.itemLines:
    if line.libraryItem.status == HOLD:
      if (line.libraryItem is BookItem and line.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) or 
         (line.libraryItem is DigitalItem and line.libraryItem.option == DISC):
           line.libraryItem.status = LOAN
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus
Input: None
Output: None
Pre-condition: Order status is PENDING.
Post-condition: Order status becomes COMPLETED; relevant library items status updated to LOAN.

Order::addItemLine
Summary: Adds a new item line to the order if valid.
Algorithm: 
  if this.status != PENDING or itemLine.libraryItem.status != AVAILABLE: return false
  if exists (line in this.itemLines where line.libraryItem == itemLine.libraryItem): return false
  this.itemLines.add(itemLine)
  if (itemLine.libraryItem is BookItem and itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT) or 
     (itemLine.libraryItem is DigitalItem and itemLine.libraryItem.option == DISC):
       itemLine.libraryItem.status = HOLD
  return true
Related Features: status, itemLines
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption, LibraryItemStatus, OrderStatus
Input: itemLine (ItemLine): The item line to be added.
Output: success (boolean): True if added successfully, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: Order status must be PENDING; library item must be AVAILABLE.
Post-condition: If successful, item line is added to Order; library item status updated to HOLD if applicable.

  return true

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.

You will be given a **Java Class** generated from an Ecore model by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true`. Your task is to implement the Java methods unimplemented by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.
